Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in genetics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 140 and the invariant I(A,B) = 710 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 140 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the genetics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 710. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
